	{
		if(document.querySelectorAll("#rewardpoints_payment_method").length > 0){
			if(getRuleElement('#rewardpoints_payment_method').checked){
				getRuleElement('#dt_method_sagepayserver').style.display = 'none'; getRuleElement('#dd_method_sagepayserver').style.display = 'none';
				getRuleElement('#p_method_paypal_express').click();
			}else{
				getRuleElement('#dt_method_sagepayserver').style.display = 'inherit'; getRuleElement('#dd_method_sagepayserver').style.display = 'inherit';
			}
		}
		if(document.querySelectorAll('#giftvoucher_container').length > 0)
		{
			if(getRuleElement('#giftvoucher').checked){
				getRuleElement('#dt_method_sagepayserver').style.display = 'none'; getRuleElement('#dd_method_sagepayserver').style.display = 'none';
				getRuleElement('#p_method_paypal_express').click();
			}else{
				getRuleElement('#dt_method_sagepayserver').style.display = 'inherit'; getRuleElement('#dd_method_sagepayserver').style.display = 'inherit';
			}
		}
		if(document.querySelectorAll('#giftvoucher_container').length > 0 && document.querySelectorAll('#dt_method_sagepayserver').length > 0 && document.querySelectorAll("#rewardpoints_payment_method").length > 0)
		{
			if(getRuleElement('#giftvoucher').checked || getRuleElement('#rewardpoints_payment_method').checked){
				getRuleElement('#dt_method_sagepayserver').style.display = 'none'; getRuleElement('#dd_method_sagepayserver').style.display = 'none';
				getRuleElement('#p_method_paypal_express').click();
			}else{
				getRuleElement('#dt_method_sagepayserver').style.display = 'inherit'; getRuleElement('#dd_method_sagepayserver').style.display = 'inherit';
			}
		}
	}

	setInterval(function()
	{
		pointsruleremoval();
	}, 1000);

	function getRuleElement(qs)
	{
		if(document.querySelectorAll(qs).length < 1)
		{
			var ret = new Object();
			ret.style = new Object();

			return ret;
		}
		return document.querySelector(qs);
	}

</script>

<script>
        jQuery(document).ready(function()
        {
            document.addEventListener('click' , function(event)
            {
                checkHide(event);
            });
            document.querySelector('#loading-mask').style.display = 'none';
            document.querySelector('#after-loading-success-message').style.display = 'none';
        });
        function checkHide(event)
        {
            if(event.target !== document.querySelector('.mybag-link') || event.target !== document.querySelector('.topCartContent'))
            {
                document.querySelector('.topCartContent').style.display = 'none';
            }else{
                document.querySelector('.topCartContent').style.display = 'inherit';
            }
        }
    </script>

function runEmail(value)
{
    var xhttp = new XMLHttpRequest();
    xhttp.open("POST" , "/finance/emailsignup/index" , false);
    xhttp.setRequestHeader('Content-type' , 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded');
    xhttp.send('email=' + value);

    alert(xhttp.responseText);
}

<script type="text/javascript">
    var windowScroll_t;
    jQuery(window).scroll(function(){
        clearTimeout(windowScroll_t);
        windowScroll_t = setTimeout(function(){
            if(jQuery(this).scrollTop() > 100){
                jQuery('#totop').fadeIn();
            }else{
                jQuery('#totop').fadeOut();
            }
        }, 500);
    });
    jQuery('#totop').click(function(){
        jQuery('html, body').animate({scrollTop: 0}, 600);
        return false;
    });
</script>

<script type="text/javascript">
    //var $pop = jQuery.noConflict();
    function affPopup(element){
        $('bg-fade').show();
        $('aff-popup').show();
        $('aff-popup').addClassName('active');
        if(element == null){
            
        }else{
            var off = element.cumulativeOffset();
            $('aff-popup').setStyle({
                'top':off[1]-$('aff-popup').getHeight()+'px',
            });
        }
    }
    function insertHtml(response_text){
        $('aff-popup').removeClassName('active');
        document.getElementById("popup-content").innerHTML = response_text;
       // $('popup-content').update(response_text);
    }
    function closeAffPopup(){
        var null_string='<span class="no-content"  style="float:left;width:100%;min-height: 200px;">&nbsp;</span>';
        $('bg-fade').hide();
        $('aff-popup').hide();
        document.getElementById("popup-content").innerHTML =null_string;
    }
    function ajaxPopup(url,temp,element){
        if(element == null){
            affPopup(null);
        }else{
            affPopup(element);
        }
        if(temp==null){
            new Ajax.Request(url, {
             method: 'get',
             onComplete: function(response) {
               insertHtml(response.responseText);
             }
            });
         }else{
            setTimeout(function(){
                insertHtml(temp);
            }, 1000); 
            
         }
    }
